Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any physiology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 313. The projective resolution has length at most 464 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 313 + O(1/n^464). Summing over all modes and applying the physiology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
